**Comparing optimizer and model hyperparameters**
回答内容（1 字符）
D
Claude 事后评审 — 盲答 D (GT E) · 可答性 3/5 · borderline
Only one reference uses RMSprop (the best at 3.1882) and at a different lr, so the winner must be inferred by extrapolating optimizer strength rather than read off a varied axis; all SGD options with lr<=3e-4 are obviously near-untrained (~ln32=3.47), making D plausible but not strictly determined.

Claude 事后评审 — 盲答 F (GT D) · 可答性 2/5 · borderline
The references only give one point per optimizer's lr curve, so the optimal learning rate for each optimizer family (esp. Adagrad 0.01 vs 0.001, RMSprop 1e-4 vs 3e-3) must be extrapolated, and several candidates plausibly land within noise of each other.

claude-opus-5 答 B vs GT B 正确
回答内容（862 字符）
**Calibration from references:**

- Wide networks (width 256) consistently land in the 0.78–0.81 range: ref 3 (depth 2, w256) = 0.783, ref 4 (depth 6, w256) = 0.787, ref 5 (depth 5, w256) = 0.811.
- Narrow networks do noticeably worse: ref 1 (width 64) = 0.866, ref 2 (width 32, residual) = 0.969. Width is the dominant factor at this low LR/short budget — small widths underfit badly, and residual connections don't rescue them (ref 2 is the worst).

**Option A:** width 16, depth 2, residual — even narrower than the worst reference (width 32). Extrapolating the width trend (256→~0.79, 64→0.87, 32→0.97), width 16 should be ≳1.0.

**Option B:** width 256, depth 5, no residual — essentially the same family as ref 5 (0.811) and ref 4 (0.787), with mixed layer norms and a similar activation mix. Expect ~0.79–0.82.

B is clearly the lower-loss setting.

**B**

claude-opus-5 答 A vs GT A 正确
回答内容（882 字符）
**Calibration from references:**

- Adagrad shows a clear trend: lr 0.1 → 0.906 (too high, diverging), lr 0.03 → 0.192 (best), lr 0.01 → 0.199, lr 0.003 → 0.289 (already degrading from being too slow). Extrapolating downward, Adagrad at lr 0.001 would be badly undertrained in 640 steps — Adagrad's accumulating denominator shrinks effective step size fast, so at lr 0.001 the model barely moves from initialization. Expect loss well above 0.29, likely ~0.6–0.9.
- RMSprop at lr 0.001 is near its standard sweet spot (RMSprop/Adam-family optima typically ~1e-3). AdamW at lr 0.1 gave 0.868 (way too high), which tells us adaptive methods here blow up at large lr but should be fine at 1e-3. Expect RMSprop lr 0.001 to land around 0.1–0.2, competitive with or better than the best Adagrad reference.

So the RMSprop setting should clearly beat the near-frozen Adagrad setting.

**A**
